## 0. File-function and controls

### 0.1 File-function

`File_68` examines the Apocalypse of Weeks as an ancient mathematical-eschatological riddle whose received ten-week form may preserve a complete seven-week core followed by a secondary three-week expansion.

The current Final source has thirteen local functions:

1. distinguish the earliest recoverable ten-week edition from the proposed earlier seven-week compositional core;
2. test whether `1 Enoch 91:8–10` preserves the older terminal tradition against which `93:9–10 + 91:11–17` may be read as an expansion;
3. show how Jeremiah's seventy years and Daniel's seventy sevens form the Week-6 / Week-7 hinge under the primary `70`-year carrier;
4. define one symbolic pre-Adam generation outside the seven weeks and open the bounded `72`-year precessional companion without making it the Danielic `490` driver;
5. preserve four fixed carrier rails while distinguishing Cainan-retained and Cainan-expunged genealogical attribution states;
6. correct Joshua I/Joshua II as two schematic positions around one historical Joshua and open the bounded Josephic double-portion comparison;
7. place the original and exact `+110` translated rails into a paired weave whose principal extended historical occupation is the MT-contoured Noah–Abraham handover and Terah–Joshua ten-row corridor;
8. distinguish the seven-unit accordion expansion from the six duplicated internal overlaps and route the `660/666–770/777` Creation-completion states without redefining the `+110` rail translation;
9. unfold the same `+110` at generation resolution so that the `70/72` cells become bounded `180/182` calendar halves and the seven-generation weeks become fully stretched `1260/1274` units, without replacing the ordinary `490/504` weeks;
10. organize the `720/728/730` local calendar signature, Jacob–Joseph midpoint envelope, Enoch-to-center repetition, and ordinary/full-stretch Flood states under one three-resolution accordion hierarchy;
11. connect the Kohath/Amram central `20|50` field to the Temple/Kings `+50`, the finite `1290→1260→1150` contraction, and the final Week-6 `606→586→536 BC` Kings/destruction/restoration closure;
12. test Flood, Jared, Temple, exile/restoration, cumulative, LXX, SKL/Berossus, and Christological locks without allowing later convergence to prove the ancient compositional history or collapse their modal states;
13. separate the Christological translation hierarchy into Joshua `21`, Enoch `49=7×7`, and Luke `76`; translate the Enochic and Joshua double-generation blocks to the Jesus block at their proper depths; execute the terminal-side inverse `−110` rail and forwarded seven-year `2|30|2` Christ envelope; and route the completed Luke/Matthew transfer to Final File_69.

The file does not adjudicate denominational eschatological systems. It also does not claim that 1 Enoch explicitly assigns `70`, `72`, `180`, or `182` ordinary years to a generation. These remain repository measurement states with distinct functions and claim ceilings.

### 0.5 Claim-status hierarchy

| Status | Function in `File_68` | Example |
|---|---|---|
| Textual datum | Directly present or securely reconstructed | Week 8 begins at `91:12`. |
| Externally source-controlled fact | Critical-edition, manuscript, or controlled chronology statement | 4Q212 preserves numerical adjacency; LXX begetting ages supply six centenary additions. |
| Arithmetic fact | Exact result under declared endpoints and operators | `7 × 72 = 504`. |
| Mechanical attribution fact | A name-position consequence on the unchanged lattice | Shelah occupies Cainan's row in the expunged overlay. |
| Strong structural inference | Best explanation of coordinated textual or arithmetic features | Joshua's two schematic rows embody the Josephic double portion. |
| Redactional hypothesis | Proposed compositional development | Weeks 8–10 expanded an earlier seven-week ending. |
| Historical hypothesis | Proposed historical origin beyond local proof | Ezra-period origin. |
| Typological / theological note | Canonical meaning that does not carry arithmetic proof | retained mortality/redemption and expunged preexistence–Incarnation readings. |
| Providential synchronization | Exact correspondence exceeding probable scribal knowledge | Mirror and deep cumulative completion. |
| Dependency-controlled | Imported from a named controller | `AD 65–72` envelope from File_13. |
| Appendix-only | Valid but unnecessary to main proof | `17 × 400` Near-Solar note. |
| Excluded / deferred | Explicitly outside current proof | historical priority of `70` versus `72`; name-by-name antediluvian residual sum; reciprocal corrections. |
| Audit note | Requires later verification or author decision | Residual cross-file audit, control registration, reciprocal routing, and publication work; historical-directionality remains optional research. |

### 0.6 Argument-control rules

The amended proof hierarchy is:

```text
textual order and literary closure
→ Jeremiah–Daniel primary-70 grammar
→ four fixed ordinary carrier rails
→ retained / expunged genealogical attribution states
→ paired-table, paired-weave, and field/week accordion architecture
→ generation-resolved `180/182` cells and `1260/1274` fully stretched weeks
→ MT-contoured Noah–Abraham / Terah–Joshua ten-row corridor
→ Christological translation hierarchy: Joshua `21`, Enoch `49=7×7`, Luke `76`, and the inverse-rail `2|30|2` / forwarded-`+7` endpoint field
→ Enoch/Flood fractal placements and Jacob–Joseph midpoint occupation
→ Kohath/Amram central `20|50` and Temple/Kings `+50`
→ finite `1290→1260→1150` contraction
→ Week-6 `606→586→536 BC` Kings/destruction/restoration closure
→ bounded cumulative, LXX, SKL/Berossus, Christological, and Mirror corroboration.
```

Controls:

1. The source-critical seven-week case cannot be proved or revised by later arithmetic.
2. The ordinary `70` and `72` carriers are applied states; neither duration is explicit in 1 Enoch.
3. The `70` carrier remains primary for Daniel's `490`; the `72` carrier remains precessional.
4. Generation-resolved `180/182` cells do not replace ordinary `70/72` generations or the exact `110` rail translation.
5. Fully stretched `1260/1274` weeks do not replace the ordinary `490/504` textual-carrier weeks.
6. Cainan expunction changes names on fixed rows; it does not move chronology.
7. Arphaxad remains fixed, and the expunged cascade stops after Joshua II.
8. Joshua I/Joshua II are schematic positions around one historical Joshua.
9. The ten-row corridor is the primary extended historical correspondence; local correlations are modules, not competing calibration systems.
10. Nominal schematic labels and historical MT occupation must remain distinct.
11. Boundary inheritance permits adjacent births, deaths, calls, and envelopes without converting rows into biological lifespans.
12. `2876/2960 BC` are Week-1 closure heads, not literal Enoch-ascent dates.
13. Ordinary `2456 BC` and fully stretched `6236/6306 BC` Flood states remain distinct from one another and from Arphaxad event heads.
14. The central `20|50` triples are mixed adjacent-row constructions and must never be presented as one native table row.
15. The Kings `+50` and the Week-6 `20|50` closure are finite; no indefinite propagation is authorized.
16. `430`, `480`, `720`, `30`, and `110` retain separate operator and state functions even when their routes commute.
17. The final Week-6 day structurally closes the Kings/destruction/restoration field but does not imply that 1 Enoch explicitly names the Judah ledger.
18. Algebraically equivalent `1290/1260/1150`, `20|50`, and `720/430` displays belong to shared evidence families.
19. MT-contour compatibility and the new Sanctuary matrix do not prove direction of textual dependence.
20. Shared coordinates do not collapse schematic, regular, cumulative, Rounded, Decimal-Invariance, SKL/Berossus, or Mirror node-classes.
21. The inherited chart's missing-pre-generation error is not the valid Cainan-expunged overlay.
22. The LXX sixfold dilation and SKL `30×50` rectangle remain bounded comparative witnesses.
23. Mirror, Near-Solar, and external SKL/Berossus material remains downstream corroboration and cannot carry the local proof.
24. Joshua `21`, Enoch `49`, and Luke `76` are distinct translation depths and must not be interchanged.
25. The terminal-side `−110` execution is the inverse application of the existing rail translation to `AD 135/137`; it does not create a fifth rail or move `AD 65`.
26. The `2+30+2=34` Christ field is a cross-rail envelope, not one ordinary `70`- or `72`-year generation.
27. `1 BC/AD 2` and `AD 32/34` are forward seven-year envelope closes. They are not carrier termini, Passion dates, or new textual weeks.
28. Planned File_69 will execute the downstream Luke/Matthew transfer. Until then, no unexecuted File_69 result may be imported into File_68.

Machine Guard [FILE_68 TEXTUAL WEEK / DUAL YEAR CARRIERS]:
1 Enoch supplies numbered weeks and a seven-generation grammar. File_68 applies `70`- and `72`-year carrier states. Do not present either ordinary-year duration as an explicit statement of 1 Enoch.

Machine Guard [FILE_68 70 / 72 CARRIER NON-COLLAPSE]:
The `70`- and `72`-year carriers are co-valid schematic states with different functions. The `70` carrier remains primary for the Jeremiah–Daniel `490`; the `72` carrier is the bounded precessional companion and produces `504`-year weeks. Neither replaces the other.

The symbolic pre-Adam generation lies outside the seven Enochic weeks. It supplies the fiftieth carrier span but does not alter `7 × 7 = 49` generations within Weeks 1–7.

Machine Guard [FILE_68 COMMON AD 65 TERMINAL]:
Both translated carrier tables terminate at `AD 65`. The final `70`-year generation is `6 BC → AD 65`; the final `72`-year generation is `8 BC → AD 65`. No `AD 67` terminal state is active. The phase-resolved `AD 64t/65n–AD 71t/72n` envelope remains distinct.

Machine Guard [FILE_68 SYMBOLIC GOD / PRE-ADAM BOOKEND]:
The pre-Adam bookend is a symbolic carrier interval outside the seven weeks. It does not assign a biological birth, age, or literal divine chronology to God.

Machine Guard [FILE_68 +110 TRANSLATED RAIL]:
The File_68-position rails terminate at `46 BC`; their exact `+110` translations terminate at `AD 65`. Every corresponding boundary and generation row must move by the same `110`. Do not mix an original head with a translated endpoint.

Machine Guard [FILE_68 GENERATION / SEVEN-YEAR ENVELOPE]:

### 0.9 Thesis

The earliest recoverable Apocalypse of Weeks was already a ten-week composition arranged in numerical order before 4Q212, but that early attestation does not settle its prior compositional history. File_68 retains as its leading reconstruction that the expanded edition may preserve a complete seven-week apocalypse whose older ending-tradition survives most clearly in `1 Enoch 91:8–10`.

Jeremiah's seventy-year land-rest field and Daniel's seventy sevens remain the canonical chronological control. Under the primary ordinary `70` carrier, the final macro-day of Week 6 is promoted into the seventh `490`-year week. The symbolic pre-Adam generation remains outside the seven weeks, the ordinary `72`-year precessional carrier remains bounded, and both translated ordinary rails retain the sole common terminal `AD 65`.

The fixed-grid and Cainan-attribution architecture remains unchanged. The MT-contoured Noah–Abraham / Terah–Joshua corridor continues to provide the primary extended historical occupation; Jacob authorizes the leading `70`, Kohath–Amram form the hidden center, and Joshua supplies the Exodus–Conquest closure.

The post-pressure-test amendment unfolds the exact `+110` translation at a third, generation-resolved scale. It executes bounded `180/182` calendar-half cells, fully stretched `1260/1274` weeks, full generated rails, and weekly gap contraction without replacing the ordinary `70/72` generations, ordinary `490/504` weeks, or `AD 65` terminal. The local `720/728/730` signature, Jacob–Joseph midpoint envelope, Enoch-to-center repetition, and ordinary/full-stretch Flood states are now arithmetically and argumentatively executed with explicit claim ceilings.

The Sanctuary/Kings layer now has one structural destination: the final Week-6 primary day `606→586→536 BC`. The Kohath/Amram central `20|50` heads, Temple/Kings `+50`, finite upper Jacob/Kings field, and translated companions are organized as a bounded `1290→1260→1150` contraction ending at that Week-6 kings/destruction/restoration closure. This is a structural correspondence with dependency-controlled Kings chronology; File_68 does not claim that 1 Enoch explicitly names the Kings of Judah ledger.

LXX/Luke, Jubilees, cumulative Shem, SKL/Berossus, Mirror, and Near-Solar material retain bounded corroborating roles. Weeks 8–10 remain an ancient and mathematically compatible expansion whose strongest role is dilation, recapitulation, or overlap—not three compulsory mortal ages after the resurrection ending of the original Week 7.

## 1. The textual riddle: composition, incorporation, and transposition

### 1.1 Three different questions

The received placement of the final weeks can be understood only if three questions are separated.

| Question | Proper object | File_68 handling |
|---|---|---|
| **Composition** | Were Weeks 8–10 part of the first form of the Apocalypse? | Open redactional question; File_68 leads with a secondary-expansion hypothesis. |
| **Incorporation** | When and how was the Apocalypse placed inside the Admonition/Epistle complex? | The ten-week Apocalypse was already copied within this complex by 4Q212. |
| **Transposition** | Why does the Geʿez chapter order place `91:11–17` before `93:1–10`? | The block was moved after the stage represented by 4Q212; motive remains debated. |

The later transposition cannot, by itself, prove that Weeks 8–10 were a later composition. Conversely, the early presence of Weeks 8–10 in 4Q212 cannot, by itself, prove that they belonged to the first compositional stage. Composition and transmission are different historical operations.

### 1.2 The Dream Visions do not extend through chapter 91

The Dream Visions proper comprise `1 Enoch 83–90`. Chapter 91 belongs to the later Admonition/Epistle complex. This correction is necessary because the inherited discussion initially described chapter 91 as the conclusion of the Dream Visions.

The critical literary map used in `File_68` is:

| Literary unit | Passage | Function |
|---|---|---|
| Dream Visions | `83–90` | Enoch’s two dream visions, including the Animal Apocalypse |
| Admonition / Exhortation | `91:1–10, 18–19` | testamentary warning and ethical two-way discourse |
| Epistle opening and body | `92:1–5; 93:11–105:2` | paraenesis, woes, vindication, and final instruction |
| Apocalypse of Weeks | `93:1–10; 91:11–17` | numbered history from Enoch’s primordial setting to the new heaven and unnumbered righteous weeks |
| Birth of Noah | `106–107` | separate narrative unit |
| Eschatological Appendix | `108` | concluding appendix |

These units were transmitted together and editorially interact, but they should not be assumed to derive from one hand or one compositional moment.

### 1.3 Received chapter order versus recovered numerical order

The received Geʿez chapter sequence is approximately:

```text
91:1–10
→ 91:11–17
→ 91:18–19
→ 92:1–5
→ 93:1–10
→ 93:11 onward.
```

This order places the future-facing completion of Week 7 and Weeks 8–10 before the historical account of Weeks 1–7.

The earlier recoverable material order is approximately:

```text
Admonition:
91:1–10, 18–19

Epistle opening:
92:1–5

Apocalypse of Weeks:
93:1–10
→ 91:11–17

Epistle continuation:
93:11 onward.
```

The internal numerical order of the Apocalypse is therefore:

```text
Weeks 1–7A:
93:3–10

Week 7B:
91:11

Weeks 8–10 and the unnumbered righteous future:
91:12–17.
```

The displaced block does not begin neatly with Week 8. It begins with `91:11`, the future completion of Week 7. This observation is central because it identifies the organizing principle of the moved block as **future eschatological material**, not merely “the last three weeks.”

### 1.4 What 4Q212 establishes

The Aramaic manuscript 4QEnᵍ / 4Q212 is damaged but decisive for order. The Vorlagen corresponding to `93:10` and `91:11` occur on consecutive lines. The expanded Apocalypse therefore circulated in numerical order before the later Geʿez transposition.

The evidence establishes the following:

| Established by the source evidence | Claim status |
|---|---|
| `93:10` was followed by material corresponding to `91:11`. | textual / codicological fact |
| `91:11` completes Week 7 before Week 8 begins at `91:12`. | critical textual fact |
| Weeks 8–10 are not a late Ethiopic invention. | strong textual conclusion |
| The ten-week expanded form existed by the first century BC. | externally source-controlled fact |
| The later received order is secondary to the numerical order. | strong transmission conclusion |

The same evidence does **not** establish:

| Not established by 4Q212 | Required classification |
|---|---|
| that Weeks 8–10 belonged to the first composition | redactional question |
| that `91:8–10` physically followed `93:10` in an earlier manuscript | unproved reconstruction |
| whether the transposition was accidental or deliberate | debated transmission hypothesis |
| whether one scribe, one school, or several hands produced the expansion | unresolved historical question |
| that every textual week equals `490` ordinary years | repository measurement, not manuscript fact |
| whether Daniel depends on Enoch or Enoch on Daniel | unresolved priority question |

The proper formula is therefore:

> 4Q212 confirms the early **ten-week edition and its numerical order**; it does not close the question of the Apocalypse’s earlier compositional growth.

### 1.5 Week 7A and Week 7B

The seventh week is divided across the transmitted chapter boundary.

#### Week 7A — `93:9–10`

The text looks back upon or locates itself within:

- an apostate generation;
- apostate deeds;
- the election of the righteous from the eternal plant;
- sevenfold instruction concerning creation.

#### Week 7B — `91:11`

The elected righteous become agents within the future resolution:

- the roots or foundations of violence are uprooted;
- deceit is destroyed;
- judgment is executed;
- sinners and blasphemers perish.

Week 8 then begins at `91:12` with the sword given to the righteous.

This division explains why a change of style is perceptible without proving a second author. The transition may first be the shift from:

```text
historical retrospect and authorial present
                     ↓
future eschatological action.
```

Nevertheless, the change also creates the exact place at which a secondary eschatological expansion could have been attached to an earlier seven-week scheme.

### 1.6 The leading File_68 compositional model

`File_68` carries the following stages as a working reconstruction:

| Stage | Proposed development | Claim status |
|---|---|---|
| **A. Older terminal tradition** | A Flood–final-judgment oracle, substantially preserved in `91:5–10`, culminates in eradication of evil, awakening, and wisdom. | strong structural inference |
| **B. Seven-week historical apocalypse** | Weeks 1–7 move from primordial history to the apostate generation and election of the righteous. The terminal tradition supplies or informs its ending. | leading redactional hypothesis |
| **C. Pre-4Q212 eschatological expansion** | `91:11–17` extends the Week-7 ending through three further weeks, the true house, universal and angelic judgment, the new heaven, and unnumbered righteous weeks. | plausible redactional hypothesis |
| **D. Early ten-week edition** | The complete expanded form circulates in numerical order and is attested by 4Q212. | textual fact |
| **E. Later transposition** | The future block `91:11–17` is relocated after `91:10`, producing the received chapter-91 sequence before Weeks 1–7 in chapter 93. | transmission fact; motive debated |

This model makes the fewest claims necessary to preserve the central observation:

> The ten-week edition is early, yet the first seven weeks may still preserve an earlier complete composition.

### 1.7 Counterevidence and claim ceiling

The secondary-expansion hypothesis must acknowledge the integration of the ten-week form.

Evidence favouring unity or sophisticated redaction includes:

- `91:11` completes Week 7 rather than beginning an unattached appendix;
- Weeks 1 and 10 both contain terminal seventh-part language in many reconstructions;
- the ten-week form begins and ends with enduring righteousness;
- Weeks 2 and 9 correlate the rise and disappearance of violence/deceit;
- the Temple of Week 5, its destruction in Week 6, and the future house in Week 8 form an integrated sanctuary arc;
- Weeks 8–10 presuppose the sinners, righteous community, failed order, and judgment developed in the earlier weeks;
- the expanded form is already ancient by the time of 4Q212.

These observations prevent a crude “foreign appendix” theory. If Weeks 8–10 are secondary, the expansion was early and compositionally intelligent. The added material may derive from the same school, a later edition by the same circle, or a redactor who understood the earlier structure closely.

The conclusion is therefore restrained:

> `File_68` does not claim that the first seven weeks are the only ancient form ever known. It argues that the ten-week edition may preserve a secondary, carefully integrated expansion of an earlier seven-week apocalypse.

## 2. The terminal oracle and the duplicated eschaton

### 2.1 The terminal tableau in `91:8–10`

In the Charles numbering used by the inherited article, the closing movement reads:

> **8.** In those days violence shall be cut off from its roots,  
> And the roots of unrighteousness together with deceit,  
> And they shall be destroyed from under heaven.
>
> **9.** And all the idols of the heathen shall be abandoned,  
> And the temples burned with fire,  
> And they shall remove them from the whole earth,  
> And they shall be cast into the judgement of fire,  
> And shall perish in wrath and in grievous judgement for ever.
>
> **10.** And the righteous shall arise from their sleep,  
> And wisdom shall arise and be given unto them.

The passage contains six terminal elements:

1. violence is cut off at the root;
2. unrighteousness and deceit are destroyed;
3. idolatry is removed;
4. pagan temples are burned;
5. the nations enter fiery and everlasting judgment;
6. the righteous awaken and receive wisdom.

The movement is not an ordinary transitional notice. It is a compressed eschatological sequence running from the destruction of evil to the awakening and illumination of the righteous.

### 2.2 The two-judgment pattern in `91:5–10`

The broader Admonition first announces a great chastisement in `91:5`. Verse 6 then says that unrighteousness will be consummated **again**. The sequence implies:

```text
first fullness of violence
→ first great chastisement
→ renewed growth of evil
→ final heavenly judgment
→ eradication of idolatry
→ awakening and wisdom.
```

The Enoch–Methuselah setting makes the Flood the natural prototype of the first judgment, although the passage does not explicitly name it. The pattern resembles Week 2:

```text
first end
→ one man saved
→ unrighteousness grows again.
```

The Admonition therefore supplies a compact Flood-to-final-judgment grammar. The first destruction does not end evil permanently; the second terminates it and awakens the righteous.

### 2.3 Why `91:10` reads as closure

The closing line joins two images that are terminal elsewhere in biblical apocalyptic:

```text
awakening from sleep
+
wisdom bestowed.
```

The first implies resurrection or eschatological vindication. The second reverses the defining failure of Week 6, where the people forsake wisdom. Together they form a complete theological resolution:

```text
wisdom forsaken
→ judgment and dispersion
→ righteous awakening
→ wisdom restored.
```

The Danielic comparison in §3 strengthens this reading, but the closure is visible before the chronology is applied. The verse does not announce another ordinary historical period. It announces the righteous order that follows the destruction of the wicked one.

Audit note:
The fragmentary Aramaic form associated with this ending may preserve additional peace/rest language. Because the reconstruction is damaged and edition-dependent, `File_68` does not make that clause proof-bearing. Appendix A records only the secure manuscript-order function.

### 2.4 The expansion matrix

The strongest evidence for an older compressed ending is the way the later Apocalypse distributes the same motifs across Week 7B and Weeks 8–10.

| `91:8–10` compressed ending | `93:9–10 + 91:11–17` expanded sequence |
|---|---|
| Violence and the roots of unrighteousness are uprooted. | Week 7B makes the elect uproot violence and the structure of deceit. |
| Idolatrous structures and pagan temples are burned. | Week 8 replaces the failed order with the house of the Great King. |
| The heathen enter fiery, everlasting judgment. | Weeks 8–10 distribute judgment through the sword, universal judgment, and angelic judgment. |
| The righteous arise from sleep. | Week 7 elects the righteous; later weeks narrate their vindication and rule. |
| Wisdom arises and is given. | Week 7 grants sevenfold instruction concerning creation. |
| Evil is removed for ever. | Week 10 brings the new heaven; `91:17` opens unnumbered weeks of righteousness. |

The relation is not a word-for-word duplicate. It is a **compression–expansion relation**:

```text
compressed terminal oracle
            ↓
distributed historical-eschatological program.
```

### 2.5 The seam at `91:10/11`

The received chapter order places `91:11` immediately after `91:10`, but the two verses have different literary provenances under the critical reconstruction.

- `91:10` concludes the Admonition’s terminal vision.
- `91:11` follows `93:10` in the Apocalypse of Weeks and depends upon the elect righteous just named there.
- `91:12` begins the eighth week.

Thus `91:11` performs an exact hinge function:

```text
Week 7 election and sevenfold instruction
                     ↓
Week 7B: the elect act against violence and deceit
                     ↓
Week 8: the sword is given to the righteous.
```

Charles enclosed verse 11 in square brackets, reflecting his pre-Qumran editorial judgment that it was an interpolation. The discovery of 4Q212 shows that substantially corresponding material was already present in the early Aramaic edition. Charles’s brackets therefore cannot be used to call the verse a late Ethiopic addition.

The more defensible conclusion is:

> `91:11` is an ancient and effective hinge in the expanded Apocalypse; its effectiveness remains compatible with its having been composed as part of a secondary pre-4Q212 expansion.

### 2.6 The duplicated-eschaton problem

If `91:8–10` is allowed its natural force, the present age has already reached its terminal resolution:

```text
evil eradicated
→ idolatry destroyed
→ everlasting fiery judgment
→ righteous awakening
→ wisdom bestowed.
```

Weeks 8–10 then appear to reopen the same final matters:

- a sword is again required;
- sinners are again delivered for judgment;
- a house is yet to be built;
- the world is again written down for destruction;
- angels are yet to be judged;
- the first heaven is yet to pass away;
- a new heaven appears only in Week 10.

Read as a strict linear sequence, this creates a second eschaton after the eschaton.

Three explanations remain possible:

| Reading | Function of Weeks 8–10 | File_68 status |
|---|---|---|
| Prolepsis | `91:8–10` announces the outcome before its stages are unfolded. | possible literary harmonization |
| Spiritual / communal awakening | the righteous “arise” within history before the final resurrection. | possible secondary rereading |
| Dilation / recapitulation | Weeks 8–10 repeat and enlarge the same consummation rather than follow it as ordinary mortal ages. | leading bounded interpretation |

The need for one of these rereadings is itself evidence that the extension is secondary to the older closure language.

### 2.7 The seven-week form is narratively complete

The first seven weeks already yield a coherent narrative:

```text
Week 1: primordial righteousness and Enoch
Week 2: violence, first end, survivor, renewed evil
Week 3: election of the righteous plant
Week 4: law and enclosure
Week 5: house of glory and dominion
Week 6: blindness, forsaken wisdom, ascent, burning, dispersion
Week 7: apostate generation, election, sevenfold instruction,
        final judgment, awakening, and wisdom.
```

Under the proposed reconstruction, the terminal material now divided between the Admonition and Apocalypse converges upon the close of Week 7. The sequence reaches:

- the destruction of the old order;
- the vindication and awakening of the righteous;
- restored wisdom;
- everlasting judgment;
- and, under the later Danielic comparison, rest and allotted inheritance.

The seven-week form is therefore not narratively unfinished. Weeks 8–10 may enrich, recapitulate, or dilate its consummation, but they are not required to supply a first ending.

### 2.8 Claim-status conclusion for §§1–2

| Proposition | Current status |
|---|---|
| The Apocalypse circulated in ten-week numerical order before the later transposition. | externally source-controlled fact |
| `91:11` completes Week 7 and Week 8 begins at `91:12`. | textual fact |
| `91:8–10` is a self-sufficient terminal tableau. | strong structural inference |
| `91:8–10` preserves the older ending-tradition used by a seven-week apocalypse. | leading redactional hypothesis |
| Weeks 8–10 are a secondary pre-4Q212 expansion. | leading but unproved redactional hypothesis |
| The later transposition deliberately paired the expanded ending with its older parallel. | plausible but unproved transmission hypothesis |
| The ten-week form is mathematically meaningless. | rejected; its mathematical and theological compatibility remains to be tested |
| The ten-week form is necessarily the first compositional form. | not established |

File_68 therefore fixes the main wording as:

> **`91:8–10` preserves the older terminal tradition**

rather than the stronger and presently unproved statement:

> **`91:8–10` physically stood after `93:10` in the first manuscript.**

## 3. Daniel 9–12, Jeremiah’s seventy years, and canonical closure

The Week-6 / Week-7 transition is not merely a numerical convenience. Its literary vocabulary is the same vocabulary that governs Daniel’s final visions.

The sixth week is defined by four coordinated failures and judgments:

1. the people become blind;
2. their hearts forsake wisdom;
3. the house of dominion is burned;
4. the chosen root is dispersed.

Daniel 9 begins from precisely that field. Daniel reads Jeremiah concerning Jerusalem’s desolations and the completion of seventy years. Gabriel then comes not merely to confirm the restoration timetable but to give Daniel **insight and understanding** and to announce seventy sevens. Daniel 12 closes the larger revelation by distinguishing the wicked from the wise, awakening those who sleep, and promising Daniel rest and an allotted inheritance.

The controlling movement is therefore:

```text
wisdom forsaken
→ Temple burned and root dispersed
→ Jeremiah’s seventy years
→ Gabriel gives insight
→ seventy sevens announced
→ the wise understand
→ sleepers awaken
→ rest and inheritance.
```

### 3.1 The Week-6 destruction field

The Enochic text says that during the sixth week the people abandon wisdom, a man ascends, the house of dominion is burned, and the chosen root is dispersed. The natural biblical comparison is the prophetic complex surrounding Jerusalem’s destruction:

- Jeremiah announces the Babylonian seventy-year field;
- Ezekiel interprets Jerusalem’s defilement, removal, and future restoration;
- Daniel begins from the desolation of Jerusalem and the exile of the covenant people;
- the restoration decree and return form the terminal boundary of the same historical field.

Under the File_68 schematic, the final seventy-year macro-day of Week 6 is:

```text
606 BC → 536 BC
= 70 years.
```

Jerusalem’s destruction in `586 BC` falls inside that closing day:

```text
606 BC → 586 BC = 20
586 BC → 536 BC = 50
20 + 50 = 70.
```

The phrase “at its conclusion” need not identify the final mathematical instant of Week 6. It may identify the closing seventy-year phase in which Babylonian domination, Temple destruction, dispersion, land rest, release, and restoration are gathered.

### 3.2 The wisdom reversal

The Week-6-to-Week-7 transition is marked by an explicit reversal of wisdom.

| Stage | Textual state | Function |
|---|---|---|
| Week 6 | the people “forsake wisdom” | apostasy and blindness |
| Daniel 9:22 | Gabriel comes `לְהַשְׂכִּילְךָ בִינָה` — “to give you insight and understanding” | revealed interpretation |
| Week 7A | the elect receive sevenfold wisdom and knowledge | restored instruction |
| Daniel 12:3 | `וְהַמַּשְׂכִּילִים יַזְהִרוּ` — “the wise shall shine” | vindication |
| Daniel 12:10 | `וְהַמַּשְׂכִּילִים יָבִינוּ` — “the wise shall understand” | terminal discernment |

Daniel therefore occupies the theological hinge demanded by the Enochic sequence:

```text
forsaken wisdom
→ revelatory understanding
→ sevenfold wisdom
→ the wise shine and understand.
```

The correspondence does not settle literary priority. It establishes that Daniel 9–12 and the proposed seven-week core participate in the same apocalyptic grammar.

### 3.3 Daniel 12 and the Week-7 ending

The correspondences are especially close at the close of the age.

| Enochic Week-7 / terminal-oracle state | Daniel 12 counterpart |
|---|---|
| an apostate or perverse generation | the wicked continue to act wickedly |
| the elect righteous are chosen | those written in the book are delivered |
| sevenfold wisdom and knowledge are given | the wise understand; knowledge increases |
| the righteous arise from sleep | those sleeping in the dust awake |
| the wicked enter everlasting judgment | everlasting life and everlasting contempt |
| righteousness and wisdom endure | the wise shine like the firmament and stars |
| final rest follows judgment | Daniel rests and stands in his allotted inheritance |

Daniel’s resurrection language is explicit:

```text
וְרַבִּים מִיְּשֵׁנֵי אַדְמַת־עָפָר יָקִיצוּ
“Many of those sleeping in the ground of dust shall awake.”
```

Daniel’s personal conclusion is equally terminal:

```text
וְתָנוּחַ וְתַעֲמֹד לְגֹרָלְךָ לְקֵץ הַיָּמִין
“You shall rest, and you shall stand for your allotted inheritance
at the end of the days.”
```

The Enochic awakening-and-wisdom conclusion and Daniel’s awakening-and-wise conclusion occupy the same literary position: the old age has reached its judgment, the righteous are vindicated, and the enduring order begins.

### 3.4 Daniel 12:5–12 is a chronometric inset

Daniel 12 should not be read as a strict sequence in which resurrection occurs in verses 1–4 and is then followed by `1290` or `1335` further days of ordinary mortal history.

The literary structure is:

```text
Daniel 12:1–4
terminal outcome announced:
distress, deliverance, awakening, wisdom, everlasting destiny

Daniel 12:5–12
“How long?”
the terminal crisis measured and its wise/wicked division restated

Daniel 12:13
Daniel's rest and standing in his lot reaffirmed.
```

The numerical dialogue turns back to measure the crisis whose outcome has already been announced. It is a chronometric inset or epilogue to the terminal vision, not a post-resurrection sequel.

This is a critical control for the Enochic problem:

> A numerical expansion may disclose the anatomy of the final crisis, but it does not require new mortal ages after the righteous have awakened and the wicked have entered everlasting judgment.

### 3.5 The `1260/1290/1335` terminal anatomy

The File_68 argument preserves the states controlled by File_17:

| Value | Status | Function |
|---:|---|---|
| `time, times, and half a time` | explicit in Daniel 12:7 | divided-final-period expression |
| `1260` | Revelation’s explicit day-equivalent; Danielic inferred companion under a `360`-day year | ideal half-week |
| `1290` | explicit in Daniel 12:11 | abomination / continual-offering measure |
| `1335` | explicit in Daniel 12:12 | blessed waiting horizon |

The arithmetic is exact:

```text
3.5 × 360 = 1260
1260 + 30 = 1290
1290 + 45 = 1335.
```

The additional `30` may be read as one ideal thirty-day intercalary month, but Daniel does not explicitly label it as such. The additional `45` carries the beatitude of the one who waits; it does not create another eschatological age.

The two Danielic starting conditions also remain distinct:

- Daniel 12:7 measures until the shattering of the holy people is completed;
- Daniel 12:11 measures from the removal of the continual offering and the setting up of the desolating abomination.

The equality of related terminal values does not require identical starting days.

### 3.6 Matthew 24 and Revelation as canonical reception

Matthew 24 joins several Danielic strands:

```text
abomination of desolation
→ unparalleled tribulation
→ cosmic disturbance
→ appearance of the Son of Man
→ trumpet and gathering of the elect.
```

The discourse combines Daniel 7, 9, 11, and 12. It does not supply a complete denominational chronology, but it does preserve the same terminal ordering: tribulation is followed by the appearing of the Son of Man and the gathering of the elect.

Revelation 20–22 provides the fuller canonical analogue:

```text
final hostile assault
→ fiery judgment
→ resurrection and judgment
→ new heaven and new earth
→ endless righteous life.
```

These canonical receptions support the natural terminal force of `1 Enoch 91:8–10`. They also explain why the later three weeks can retain force only as an unfolding, recapitulation, or interim, not as three ordinary mortal cycles after final resurrection.

### 3.7 Danielic conclusion

The source-critical and canonical evidence now converge:

> Week 6 ends in the loss of wisdom, Temple destruction, and dispersion. Daniel begins within that destruction-and-dispersion field, receives understanding, and announces seventy sevens. Daniel 12 then closes with the wise, awakening, everlasting destiny, rest, and inheritance. The Danielic numbers measure the terminal trouble; they do not reopen history after its resurrection ending.

This conclusion strengthens the leading reconstruction that `91:8–10` preserves the older terminal tradition of a complete seven-week apocalypse.

## 4. The Jeremiah–Daniel Sabbath-Promotion theorem

The chronological hinge can now be executed.

### 4.1 The primary `420 + 70 = 490` constitution

The primary Sabbath architecture is not `490 + 70`. It is:

```text
420 + 70 = 490.
```

The ideal covenantal constitution consists of seventy ordinary septennial cycles:

```text
70 × (6 work years + 1 Sabbath year)
= 70 × 7
= 490 years.
```

Their category totals are:

```text
70 × 6 = 420 work years
70 × 1 = 70 Sabbath years
420 + 70 = 490.
```

The seventy Sabbath years should have been distributed throughout the `490`, one in each seven-year cycle. The Enochic macro-week reorganizes the same proportions as six seventy-year macro-days followed by one seventy-year Sabbath macro-day:

```text
6 × 70 + 1 × 70
= 420 + 70
= 490.
```

This macro-display does not claim that ideal Israelite practice consisted of `420` consecutive work years followed by `70` consecutive rest years. It displays the distributed `6 + 1` constitution at seventy-year scale.

### 4.2 The hidden `×10` septennial grammar

Each seventy-year Enochic generation silently contains ten ordinary seven-year cycles:

```text
70 years
= 10 × 7
= 10 × (6 + 1)
= 60 work years + 10 Sabbath years.
```

Across seven Enochic generations:

```text
7 × (60 + 10)
= 420 + 70
= 490.
```

If the Sabbath pattern is consistently violated, the debt count may also be displayed as:

```text
420 chronological years contain 60 septennial cycles
final 70 chronological years contain 10 more
60 + 10 = 70 missed Sabbath years.
```

The regrouping:

```text
420 + (60 + 10)
= 480 + 10
= 490
```

is an arithmetic anatomy of the final macro-day, not a second chronological sequence. The driving File_68 pattern remains the ideal `420 + 70 = 490` constitution.

### 4.3 Week 6 on the first-decree rail

Under the `536 BC` restoration state, Week 6 is:

```text
1026 BC → 606 BC = 420
606 BC → 536 BC  = 70
1026 BC → 536 BC = 490.
```

The final macro-day contains the destruction and recovery field:

```text
606 BC → 586 BC = 20
586 BC → 536 BC = 50
606 BC → 536 BC = 70.
```

Thus the Week-6 close is both:

- the seventh seventy-year day of one Enochic `490`;
- the historical field in which the Temple is burned, the root dispersed, the land rests, and restoration begins.

The destruction at `586 BC` is not displaced merely because `536 BC` is the mathematical boundary. It occupies the closing day named by the Enochic text.

### 4.4 Jeremiah’s seventy years and Gabriel’s seventy sevens

Daniel 9 first names:

```text
שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה
“seventy years.”
```

Gabriel then announces:

```text
שָׁבֻעִים שִׁבְעִים
“seventy sevens.”
```

The scale is promoted by seven:

```text
Jeremiah:
70 years

Gabriel:
70 × 7 years
= 490 years.
```

In Enochic terms:

```text
one Enochic day
= 70 years

one Enochic week
= 7 × 70 years
= 490 years.
```

Daniel asks at the completion of the final day of Week 6. Gabriel answers by revealing the full duration of Week 7.

### 4.5 The factor reversal

The Enochic and Danielic systems factor the same `490` in reverse:

| System | Number of units | Length of unit | Total |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Enochic chronological week | `7` generations | `70` years | `490` |
| Danielic seventy weeks | `70` weeks | `7` years | `490` |

Therefore:

```text
7 × 70
=
70 × 7
=
490.
```

This factor reversal is the central mathematical interface of File_68. Daniel’s entire seventy-week prophecy occupies the volume of one Enochic week under the primary `70`-year carrier.

### 4.6 The macro-Sabbath recursion

The same `6 + 1` pattern operates at two scales.

#### Within Week 6

```text
6 × 70 + 1 × 70
= 420 + 70
= 490.
```

#### Across the original seven-week apocalypse

```text
6 × 490 + 1 × 490
= 2940 + 490
= 3430.
```

The ratios are identical:

```text
70 / 490 = 1/7
490 / 3430 = 1/7.
```

The seventh day of Week 6 is therefore the microcosm of the seventh week of the whole apocalypse.

### 4.7 The sixth week as prototype of the seventh

The close of Week 6 provides the historical pattern:

```text
distress and apostasy
→ Temple destroyed
→ chosen root dispersed
→ measured seventy-year field
→ release and restoration.
```

The close of Week 7 magnifies the pattern:

```text
apostate generation
→ unparalleled distress
→ deliverance of the written elect
→ sleepers awaken
→ wise receive understanding
→ everlasting life, rest, and inheritance.
```

The historical destruction-and-restoration event is therefore the prototype of the terminal judgment-and-resurrection event.

### 4.8 The initial decree state

The historical decree and restoration field is approximately `538–536 BC`. File_68 uses `536 BC` as the exact arithmetic member because:

```text
536 BC → 46 BC
= 490 years.
```

This is the first-decree or initial-restoration execution of Daniel’s `490` within File_68. Later decree heads may represent delays, renewals, or alternate executions of the same seventh-week prophecy. They are not automatically the eighth, ninth, and tenth Enochic weeks.

### 4.9 The Jeremiah–Daniel Sabbath-Promotion theorem

> The sixth Enochic week is a `490`-year macro-week whose ideal constitution is `420 + 70`: seventy septennial cycles contain `420` work years and `70` Sabbath years. Under the initial restoration state, Week 6 runs from `1026 BC` to `536 BC`, and its final seventy-year macro-day runs from `606 BC` to `536 BC`, containing Jerusalem’s destruction in `586 BC`. Daniel first contemplates Jeremiah’s seventy years and then receives Gabriel’s seventy sevens. The scale is promoted from one Enochic day to one Enochic week: `70 × 7 = 490`. Since six Enochic weeks have elapsed, this announced `490` is naturally the seventh week. The same Sabbath proportion therefore operates as `420 + 70 = 490` and `2940 + 490 = 3430`.

The chronological head is not an isolated `3476 BC` point. It is a Creation-style seven-year envelope carrying both Tishri and Nisan phase labels:

```text
3477t–3470t BC

3477t/3476n–3470t/3469n BC.
```

```text
E_k = E₀ − 490k
```

component-wise. The seven-year width and the Tishri/Nisan relation are preserved.

| After Week 7 | `3430` | `47t/46n–40t/39n BC` | first-decree terminal envelope |

The third-week distinction is important. The text identifies Abraham as the elected man and his posterity as the enduring plant. The arithmetic boundary lands on Jacob / Israel. File_68 therefore distinguishes:

- **chronological corporate landing:** Jacob / Israel, the embodied posterity.

and:

The complete phase state must be retained whenever component-wise arithmetic is active.

The arithmetic of the rail is exact once the local schematic head and the `490` carrier are declared. The historical meaning of each intermediate boundary varies by node-class. File_68 does not claim that every boundary is an independently dated event in 1 Enoch. The rail is a generated chronological scaffold whose validity must be tested against independent repository anchor families.

## 6. The symbolic pre-Adam bookend and the bounded dual carriers

### 6.1 The outer fiftieth span

The seven textual weeks contain:

```text
7 × 7
= 49 generations.
```

The corrected schematic places one symbolic carrier interval before Adam:

```text
1 pre-generation
+ 49 generations inside Weeks 1–7
= 50 carrier spans.
```

The bookend lies outside the numbered weeks. It therefore restores Adam to the first position of Week 1 and Enoch to the seventh without creating a Week 0 or an eighth textual week.

### 6.2 The four exact rail heads

| `72` | `3646 BC` | `3574 BC` | `46 BC` | `3536 BC` | `3464 BC` | `AD 65` |

```text
= 70,

= 72.
```

The seven-week bodies are exact:

```text
= 3430
= 49 × 70
= 7 × 490,

= 3528
= 49 × 72
= 7 × 504.
```

The complete pre-head-to-terminal volumes are:

```text
= 3500
= 50 × 70,

3646 − 46
= 3600
= 50 × 72.
```

The translated forms preserve the same volumes under civil no-year-zero arithmetic:

```text

= 3436 + 65 − 1
= 3500,

3536 BC → AD 65
= 3536 + 65 − 1
= 3600.
```

### 6.3 Exact `+110` translation and the common terminal

The two terminal positions are separated by:

```text
46 BC → AD 65
= 46 + 65 − 1
= 110.
```

The same translation operates at every source head:

```text
3546 − 3436 = 110
3476 − 3366 = 110
3646 − 3536 = 110
3574 − 3464 = 110.
```

Both translated tables terminate at `AD 65`. Their final generations are:

```text
= 70,

8 BC → AD 65
= 72.
```

The historical `6 BC` Christological hinge therefore begins the final `70`-year generation and lies two years inside the final `72`-year generation:

```text
8 BC → 6 BC
= 2
= 72 − 70.
```

No active `AD 67` terminal remains. The separate phase-resolved envelope `AD 64t/65n–AD 71t/72n` remains a seven-year terminal envelope, not a `72`-year-generation endpoint.

### 6.3A The common terminal as Jesus double-generation hinge

The common `AD 65` terminus also serves as the hinge between two bounded Christological schematic generations. Under the primary carrier:

```text
6 BC → AD 65
= 70,

AD 65 → AD 135
= 70,

6 BC → AD 135
= 140
= 2 × 70.
```

Under the companion carrier:

```text
8 BC → AD 65
= 72,

AD 65 → AD 137
= 72,

8 BC → AD 137
= 144
= 2 × 72.
```

`File_43` preserves the Luke-derived `AD 65–AD 135` final display row. File_68 uses that row as the primary Jesus double-generation block and adds the exact `72`-carrier companion through `AD 137`. The Luke-derived `76`-generation corresponding-boundary translation and the Enochic `49`-generation translation are distinct and are executed separately in §11.2A–C.

The node classes remain controlled. `AD 65` is still the sole translated terminus of the seven-week lattice. `AD 135/137` are post-terminal second-generation closes used for the double-generation comparison; they are not Christ's death dates, new eschatological termini, or additional weeks in the textual Apocalypse.

### 6.3B The inverse `−110` Christ envelope and the forwarded seven-year closes

The existing rail translation has a bounded terminal-side inverse. Applying `−110` to the generated second-generation closes gives:

```text
AD 135 − 110
= AD 25,

AD 137 − 110
= AD 27.
```

The no-rail Jesus heads and the inverse-rail closes therefore form one exact Christological envelope:

```text
8 BC → 6 BC → AD 25 → AD 27
   2        30         2

2 + 30 + 2
= 34.
```

The middle span is primary:

```text
6 BC → AD 25
= 6 + 25 − 1
= 30.
```

The companion carrier supplies the two-year flanks:

```text
8 BC → 6 BC
= 2,

AD 25 → AD 27
= 2.
```

The separate seven-year Creation envelope is then forwarded to every endpoint. Under the no-rail heads:

```text
8 BC + 7 elapsed years
= 1 BC,

6 BC + 7 elapsed years
= AD 2.
```

Under the inverse-rail heads:

```text
AD 25 + 7
= AD 32,

AD 27 + 7
= AD 34.
```

Thus the furthest week-of-years endpoints are:

```text
no rail:       1 BC / AD 2
inverse rail:  AD 32 / AD 34.
```

They preserve the same outer envelope:

```text
1 BC → AD 2 → AD 32 → AD 34
  2         30         2

2 + 30 + 2
= 34.
```

The `−110` execution is a local inverse endpoint projection, not a fifth ordinary rail. The `+7` execution advances the separate seven-year envelope; it does not lengthen the `70/72` generations. `AD 25/27` and `AD 32/34` may coincide with independently controlled Christological comparison fields, but that agreement does not collapse their node classes or make them alternate carrier termini.

### 6.4 Why both carriers are retained

The `70` carrier remains primary because it is the authorized Jacobean generation and because it produces the Jeremiah–Daniel week:

```text
7 × 70
= 70 × 7
= 490.
```

The `72` carrier is the standard precessional companion:

```text
72 × 360
= 25920.
```

Its seven-generation week is:

```text
7 × 72
= 504.
```

The duality also belongs to the inherited nations-and-mission tradition:

- the Hebrew/MT Table of Nations is conventionally counted as `70`;
- the LXX companion tradition yields `72`;
- Luke 10:1 and 10:17 preserve ancient manuscript readings of both `70` and `72`.

For source control, File_68 follows the SBLGNT apparatus and Nathan C. Johnson's reception-historical study, which notes that the early papyri themselves divide: P45 supports `70`, while P75 supports `72`. Co-validity within the inherited symbolic tradition does not establish which reading or carrier was historically prior.

### 6.5 Pre-Adamic analogies and claim ceiling

Luke's figurative God bookend and the SKL Pre-Creation field provide structural analogies for an outer pre-Adamic interval. They do not prove direct literary dependence and do not assign a biological age or birth chronology to God.

The resulting theorem is bounded:

> **Pre-Adam Dual-Carrier Theorem.** The seven Enochic weeks begin with Adam and contain forty-nine generations. One symbolic pre-generation outside the seven weeks produces fifty complete carrier spans. Under the `70` carrier the outer volume is `3500`; under the `72` carrier it is `3600`. The same four rails occupy File_68 position at `46 BC` and translated Lukan position at the common `AD 65` terminal.

### 6.6 Cainan-retained and Cainan-expunged attribution states

The four carrier rails are invariant. File_68 now distinguishes two valid genealogical-attribution states on those fixed rows:

- **Cainan retained:** the main LXX/Luke-style named order remains displayed in §7 and Appendix C, with Joshua I and Joshua II corrected as two successive schematic positions.
- **Cainan expunged:** Arphaxad remains fixed; Shelah occupies Cainan's row; every later genealogical name advances one row through Joshua II; the following row remains genealogically unassigned.

The expunged overlay is a covenantal/theological attribution state modeled on File_53. It is not a subtraction of `70`, `72`, `130`, or `460`, and it is not the inherited chart's missing-pre-generation error. The pre-Adam bookend remains symbolic under both states and supplies no literal divine age.

Detailed arithmetic consequences are executed in §§8–11. Section 7 establishes the human-facing attribution control; Appendix C supplies the complete machine-checkable overlay.

## 9A. The MT-Contoured Noah–Abraham Handover and Terah–Joshua Ten-Row Corridor

### 9A.1 Nominal schematic corridor and historical MT occupation

Second Cainan's expunction does not create a new chronology. It advances the genealogical occupants across ten fixed rows so that the named schematic corridor becomes:

```text
Terah
Abraham
Isaac
Jacob
Levi
Kohath
Amram
Moses/Aaron
Joshua I
Joshua II.
```

The historical correspondence begins one generation earlier in narrative function. The first expunged Terah heads are occupied by Noah's MT death field, and the upper Noah-death member is also Abraham's birth. The corridor closes in the Exodus–Conquest field. Two layers must therefore remain explicit:

| Layer | Opening | Closing |
|---|---|---|
| Nominal schematic corridor | Terah head after Cainan expunction | Joshua-II close |
| Historical MT occupation | Noah's death / Abraham's birth and age `60` | Exodus / Conquest |

The schematic labels identify the active succession rows. The MT events show how those rows are historically occupied. The two layers are coordinated but must not be collapsed into a list of biological lifespans.

### 9A.6 Boundary-inheritance grammar

The correspondences operate through a small number of repeated modes:

| Mode | Function | Principal examples |
|---|---|---|
| Death-to-head inheritance | a predecessor's death occupies a successor's schematic head | Noah death at Nahor/Terah head |
| Begetting-age transposition | parental begetting ages become successor-biography ages | Terah `70/130` → Abraham ages `70/130` |
| Envelope completion | carried `+5/+7` reaches call, death, or rest boundary | Terah deaths; Jacob final `70`; Judah/Levi deaths |
| Household succession | one row contains movement among adjacent family heads | Ishmael → Isaac → Jacob |
| Representative tribal occupation | one row carries a brother or tribal branch | Levi field carrying Judah's `129` |
| Hidden-center occupation | fixed boundaries mark textually silent generations | Kohath–Amram |
| Double-row absorption | duplicated successor field absorbs the Cainan shift | Joshua I / Joshua II |

A schematic row therefore functions as a succession field. It need not equal the complete biological lifespan of its nominal occupant. The repeated inheritance of births, deaths, calls, and transition boundaries is the corridor's governing grammar.

### 9A.7 MT-contour control and the roles of the other traditions

The proof-bearing historical occupation of this corridor is led by the MT. The Enochic schematic hugs the MT contour from Noah's death and Abraham's birth through Jacob, the hidden Levitical sector, the Exodus, and the Conquest.

The other traditions retain bounded functions:

| Tradition | Function in File_68 |
|---|---|
| MT | historical and biographical occupation of the ten-row corridor |
| LXX/Luke | preservation of second Cainan and an independent sixfold-centenary dilation analogy |
| Book of Jubilees | historical retention but covenantal expunction of Cainan; theological precedent for the two attribution states |
| SP | secondary Creation-wrapper and variant comparison, including the separate `4206–4199 BC` state |
| `72` carrier | precessional companion and exact two-year corridor state; not an LXX historical chronology |

Shared labels do not erase node-class or tradition. The corridor is MT-contoured without making every comparison coordinate an MT textual date.

### 9A.8 Historical-direction hypothesis and claim ceiling

The unified corridor makes a historical-development hypothesis plausible:

> An older Enochic-type equal-generation template may have been biographically naturalized in the MT by redistributing fixed schematic boundaries into begetting ages, births, deaths, calls, hidden generations, Exodus, and Conquest.

The arithmetic does not establish direction. Four models remain open:

1. Enochic-type schematic → MT biographical chronology;
2. MT chronology → Enochic abstraction;
3. development from a common chronographic ancestor;
4. providential convergence beyond demonstrable literary dependence.

The file may state exact compatibility and a shared chronographic grammar. It must not claim that the extant text of `1 Enoch 91–93` directly produced Genesis or that borrowing direction is proved.

### 9A.9 Terah–Joshua Correspondence-Corridor theorem

> **MT-Contoured Noah–Abraham Handover and Terah–Joshua Corridor Theorem.** With second Cainan expunged, ten consecutive fixed rows become Terah through Joshua II. Their translated `70` rail is `2106–1406 BC=700`, exactly the MT field from Noah's base death and Abraham age `60` to the Conquest. Their translated `72` rail is `2168–1448 BC=720`, the exact two-year companion of the MT `2166–1446 BC=720` field from Noah's `+60` death and Abraham's birth to the Exodus. The carrier difference `20` is also the reduction `60−40` from the patriarchal opening to the Exodus–Conquest close. The corridor forms five two-generation modules and exact `5+5` halves at the retained-Levi / expunged-Kohath boundary, where the MT passes from variant biography into the hidden Kohath–Amram sector and fixed Mosaic chronology. Its local correlations are therefore executions of one succession-relay system rather than competing attempts to identify a single best match.

## 11. Luke, Matthew, Joshua, Shem, and the common `AD 65` closure

`AD 65` is the sole translated generational terminal for both carriers. The `70`-year final generation is `6 BC → AD 65`; the `72`-year final generation is `8 BC → AD 65`. The phase-resolved `AD 64t/65n–AD 71t/72n` envelope remains a separate seven-year state.

## 12. What remains of Weeks 8–10

The source-critical argument does not require the later three weeks to be meaningless. It requires their role to be defined more carefully.

### 12.1 The correct count

The original seven-week structure contains:

```text
7 weeks × 7 generations
= 49 generations.
```

The three added weeks contain:

```text
3 weeks × 7 generations
= 21 generations.
```

The expanded ten-week edition therefore contains:

```text
49 + 21
= 70 generations.
```

The inherited phrase `70 + 30 generations` is incorrect and is retired. The Enochic count is therefore `49=7×7`; the added three-week field is `21=3×7`; Luke’s distinct corresponding-boundary count is `76` and must not be substituted for either Enochic count.

### 12.2 Why the seven-week form is complete

The seven-week form closes simultaneously as:

- seven textual weeks;
- forty-nine generations;
- a Sabbath-of-Sabbaths or Jubilee-square structure;
- the destruction of evil;
- resurrection or awakening from sleep;
- restored wisdom;
- Danielic rest and allotted inheritance;
- one complete `3430 = 7 × 490` chronological volume.

It is not a fragment requiring three more weeks to acquire an ending.

### 12.3 The duplicated-eschaton constraint

If Weeks 8–10 are read as strict linear successors to `91:8–10`, the resulting order is difficult:

```text
final fiery judgment
→ righteous awakening and wisdom
→ three further ages containing sinners, swords, judgments,
  temple construction, angelic judgment, and a new heaven.
```

Daniel 12 provides the canonical control. The terminal measures explain the crisis leading to resurrection; they do not create new ordinary ages after resurrection.

### 12.4 The bounded legitimate functions

Weeks 8–10 may function as:

1. **proleptic unfolding** — the final outcome is announced first and then narrated in stages;
2. **communal awakening** — the righteous community arises historically before the final universal resurrection;
3. **messianic interim** — initial election and victory are separated from final cosmic manifestation;
4. **recapitulation** — the later weeks repeat and enlarge the same consummation rather than follow it linearly;
5. **overlapping chronological field** — a valid three-week volume begins earlier and terminates at the same AD horizon.

These readings preserve the theological force of the added material without making it the original ending.

### 12.5 The authentic overlapping `3 × 490` field

A true three-week terminal field exists:

```text
1406 BC → AD 65
= 1406 + 65 − 1
= 1470
= 3 × 490.
```

Section 9F.4A now shows that this equality is the middle member of the complete primary Joshua–Jesus boundary translation `1476/1406/1336 BC → 6 BC/AD 65/AD 135`, and supplies the bounded companion `3 × 504 = 1512` translation. This is the Joshua `21=3×7` generation layer. It must not be confused with the Enochic `49=7×7` seven-week translation or Luke’s `76`-generation corresponding-boundary translation.

But this field begins at the Conquest, not after `46 BC`.

It overlaps the original Week-7 field:

```text
Conquest 1406 BC
        ↓ 1470 = 3 × 490
AD 65

Restoration 536 BC
        ↓ 490
46 BC
        ↓ 110
AD 65.
```

The later redactor may therefore have preserved a legitimate Conquest-to-consummation volume while presenting it as a linear sequel to an already complete ending.

### 12.6 Mathematical compatibility versus compositional originality

Once the unit `490` is fixed, adding three further weeks automatically preserves the arithmetic lattice. The fact that Weeks 8–10 can be measured as `3 × 490` proves compatibility, not originality.

The evidence must remain ordered:

```text
literary closure and seam
→ source-critical reconstruction
→ arithmetic compatibility
→ providential synchronization.
```

Arithmetic may show that the redactor understood or inherited the right grammar. It cannot by itself prove that the expansion belonged to the first composition.

### 12.7 Bounded conclusion concerning the extension

> The later three weeks are ancient, mathematically coherent, and theologically useful. Under the leading File_68 reconstruction, however, they are secondary to a complete seven-week core. Their strongest role is to dilate, recapitulate, or overlap the messianic consummation. They do not require three ordinary mortal ages after the resurrection and everlasting judgment announced at the original Week-7 close.

## 13. Conclusion

The source-critical heart of File_68 remains unchanged. The earliest recoverable Apocalypse remains a ten-week edition; the proposed seven-week core remains a redactional reconstruction supported by literary closure, `91:8–10`, the duplicated-eschaton problem, and the protected Jeremiah–Daniel Sabbath-Promotion proof.

The fixed architecture now operates at three accordion resolutions. At field resolution the complete forty-nine-generation body receives one `+110`; at week resolution each of seven weeks receives one; at generation resolution every patriarchal cell receives one. The finest resolution yields:

```text
70 + 110 = 180 = 360 ÷ 2
72 + 110 = 182 = 364 ÷ 2,
```

and therefore:

```text
7×180=1260
7×182=1274.
```

The generated `8820/8918` fields converge at the same ordinary `AD 65` terminal because the weekly `14` difference exhausts the initial `98` carrier difference in seven steps. No ordinary `70/72` generation, `490/504` week, carrier head, or terminal has moved.

At the terminal hinge, Jesus receives the same bounded double-generation form already identified for Joshua. The primary Joshua and Jesus blocks correspond by `1470=21×70=3×490`; the companion blocks correspond by `1512=21×72=3×504`. This is the Joshua `21`-generation or three-week-overlap layer.

The deeper Enochic translation is `49=7×7` generations. The primary Enochic block `3436→3366→3296 BC` maps boundary-for-boundary to `6 BC→AD 65→AD 135` by `3430=49×70`; the companion `3536→3464→3392 BC` maps to `8 BC→AD 65→AD 137` by `3528=49×72`. Luke’s figurative God/Adam opening forms the distinct `76`-generation corresponding-boundary translation: `5326 BC→6 BC = 5256 BC→AD 65 = 5186 BC→AD 135 = 5320=76×70`. The reason this resolves as `76` rather than `77` is developed in Final File_69.

The terminal-side inverse rail carries `AD 135/137` back by `110` to `AD 25/27`. Paired with the no-rail heads `8/6 BC`, the Christological field is `8 BC→6 BC→AD 25→AD 27 = 2+30+2=34`. Forwarding the separate seven-year Creation envelope gives the furthest week-of-years endpoints `1 BC/AD 2` and `AD 32/34`, preserving the same `2+30+2` outer form. None of these relations moves `AD 65` from its seven-week terminal node-class.

The local `720/728/730` triptych gives the calendar theorem historical occupation. Its Enochian `364+364` midpoint falls on the Jacob–Joseph envelope `1922–1915 BC`, and the exact `110` rail translation carries that full envelope to Joseph's terminal week `1812–1805 BC`. The expanded Adam heads also repeat `1260/1274` through the Enoch / Week-1 closure heads to the Kohath–Amram hidden center.

The Flood now has two declared resolution states. In the ordinary lattice `2456 BC` is the dual-carrier Week-2 closure junction: the companion `72` week closes there, while the primary `70` carrier begins its final Week-2 generation there and closes at `2386 BC`. At generation resolution the Week-2 boundaries become `6236/6306 BC`, landing `4830/4900` before the Conquest. Arphaxad's Flood-event/post-Flood genealogical role remains distinct from both cycle-boundary states.

The principal downstream closure is the Sanctuary/Kings `20|50` matrix. The translated Kohath/Amram central pair `1736/1686 BC` carries a `50` that contracts natively to the House-of-glory `30`; the independent Temple-palace `20=7+13` restores the Kings `50` at `1016/966 BC`. Uniform `720` and `430` descents carry the central pair through the Temple to `586/536 BC`, producing `1150`; restoring the File_68-position `+110` produces `1260`.

The wider finite matrix preserves `20|50` at every level:

```text
1896/1876/1826
→ 1866/1846/1796
→ 1756/1736/1686
→ 1036/1016/966
→ 606/586/536 BC.
```

Its contraction anatomy is:

```text
1290 = 30 + 110 + 720 + 430
1260 =      110 + 720 + 430
1150 =            720 + 430.
```

The final primary day of Week 6 is therefore not an isolated `606–536 BC` span. Its internal `606→586→536=20+50` is the lower row of the same finite Kings field, closing the schematic kings/destruction/Babylonian-bondage/restoration period at the independently authorized termini of the Kings `+50`. The upper Levi/Kohath/Amram field marks the distinct Egyptian-bondage interface; the shared values do not collapse the two historical bondages.

The principal extended historical correspondence remains the MT-contoured Noah–Abraham handover and Terah–Joshua corridor. LXX/Luke supplies second Cainan and an independent dilation analogy; Jubilees supplies the retained/expunged-Cainan precedent; the `72` rail remains a bounded precessional companion. Cumulative Shem, Decimal Invariance, and SKL/Berossus remain bounded corroboration. Mirror remains appendix-only. File_68 is substantively concluded at this stage. Final File_69 transfers these principles to Luke and Matthew; later File_68 change should normally be limited to bounded reciprocal maintenance unless a genuine contradiction emerges.

The later three weeks remain ancient and mathematically compatible. Under the leading reconstruction, however, they retain only bounded force as dilation, recapitulation, or overlapping messianic-interim structure—not as three compulsory mortal ages after the resurrection ending of the original Week 7.

The August 13 Final retains renewed independent certification over `1581` arithmetic rows and `453` claim controls. The concluding August 15 amendment is separately focused verified through `62/62` arithmetic controls and `16/16` claim controls. The author-invoked Finalization refresh remains controlling for the inherited source; no new full Pressure Test is claimed for the concluding replacement.

## Appendix A. Textual-order and redactional source matrix

### A.1 Literary units

| Unit | Reconstructed extent | Function |
|---|---|---|
| Dream Visions | `1 Enoch 83–90` | independent dream-vision collection |
| Admonition / Exhortation | `91:1–10,18–19` | ethical-testamentary warning and terminal judgment tableau |
| Epistle opening | `92:1–5` | introduction to the epistolary complex |
| Apocalypse of Weeks | `93:1–10; 91:11–17` | ten numbered weeks in numerical order |
| Epistle continuation | `93:11–105:2` | continuation of the Epistle proper |

### A.2 Received and recoverable orders

| State | Order | Claim status |
|---|---|---|
| received Geʿez chapter order | `91:1–10 → 91:11–17 → 91:18–19 → 92 → 93:1–10 → 93:11 onward` | transmitted order |
| Milik/Stuckenbruck recoverable sequence | `91:1–10,18–19 → 92:1–5 → 93:1–10 → 91:11–17 → 93:11 onward` | critical reconstruction supported by 4Q212 |
| proposed earlier compositional core | Weeks 1–7 with an older terminal tradition substantially preserved in `91:8–10` | File_68 redactional hypothesis |

### A.3 4Q212 control

The Aramaic manuscript 4Q212 preserves portions of the Admonition, Epistle, and Apocalypse. The key source-critical observation is that the Aramaic counterparts of `93:10` and `91:11` occur consecutively in fragment 1, column iv. The surviving lines around the seam include:

- `4Q212 1 iv 12–13`: the elect from the eternal plant of righteousness;
- the following lines: their action against violence and deceit;
- the eighth week begins after the Week-7 action.

The manuscript is fragmentary. It establishes numerical adjacency in the expanded Apocalypse but does not preserve the unattested first composition.

### A.4 Week 7A / Week 7B / Week 8

| Segment | Reference | Function |
|---|---|---|
| Week 7A | `93:9–10` | apostate generation; election; sevenfold wisdom and knowledge |
| Week 7B | `91:11` | future uprooting of violence and deceit; execution of judgment |
| Week 8 | `91:12–13` | sword of the righteous; judgment; true house / temple |

The displaced block therefore begins with the future completion of Week 7, not simply with Week 8.

### A.5 Compression–expansion ledger

| Older terminal tradition in `91:8–10` | Expanded Apocalypse |
|---|---|
| roots of violence and unrighteousness removed | Week 7B uproots violence and deceit |
| pagan structures burned | Week 8 constructs the true house |
| heathen enter fiery everlasting judgment | Weeks 8–10 distribute earthly, angelic, and cosmic judgment |
| righteous awaken | elect community is chosen and vindicated |
| wisdom arises and is given | sevenfold wisdom and knowledge are given |
| enduring righteous order | new heaven and unnumbered righteous weeks |

### A.6 Claim-status ledger

| Proposition | Status |
|---|---|
| ten-week numerical order predates the later transposition | externally source-controlled fact |
| Week 7 is split between `93:9–10` and `91:11` | textual / critical fact |
| `91:8–10` is terminal closure language | strong structural inference |
| `91:8–10` preserves the older seven-week ending-tradition | leading redactional hypothesis |
| Weeks 8–10 are a pre-4Q212 secondary expansion | leading but unproved redactional hypothesis |
| the later transposition deliberately paired two related endings | plausible transmission hypothesis |
| Enoch depends on Daniel, Daniel on Enoch, or both on common tradition | unresolved |

## Appendix D. First-day and secondary extension comparisons

### D.1 First day of the eighth cycle

This post-terminal fiftieth-generation display is distinct from the new symbolic pre-Adam generation. The former follows Week 7; the latter precedes Adam outside Weeks 1–7. They must not be collapsed merely because each can be expressed as `49 + 1 = 50`.

The first seventy-year day after the `46 BC` Nisan head is:

```text
46 BC → AD 25
= 46 + 25 − 1
= 70.
```

From the `3476 BC` head:

```text
3476 BC → AD 25
= 3476 + 25 − 1
= 3500
= 50 × 70.
```

This creates the secondary Jubilee display:

```text
49 generations
+ 1 generation
= 50 generations.
```

It is a useful messianic first-day observation but is not required for the principal File_68 proof.

### D.2 Exodus-to-ministry companion

```text
1446 BC → AD 25
= 1446 + 25 − 1
= 1470
= 3 × 490.
```

This is a distinct Exodus-side `1470` carrier and must not be collapsed with the Conquest-to-`AD 65` three-week field.

### D.3 `AD 445` secondary week

A further `490` from the first-decree terminus gives:

```text
46 BC → AD 445
= 46 + 445 − 1
= 490.
```

Thus:

```text
536 BC → AD 445
= 980
= 2 × 490.
```

The `AD 445` landing is a providential counterpart to the `445 BC` Nehemiah decree label. It is not a formal Mirror proof and does not establish the originality of Week 8.

### D.4 First-day corridor samples

| Window | Span | Internal display |
|---|---:|---|
| `2496 → 2426 BC` | `70` | selected Flood-side `40 + 30` display |
| `2006 → 1936 BC` | `70` | Jacob birth to cumulative Jacob/Levi interface |
| `1516 → 1446 BC` | `70` | `40 + 30` through Joshua birth and Exodus |
| `1026 → 956 BC` | `70` | `10 + 50 + 10` sanctuary field |
| `536 → 466 BC` | `70` | `20 + 30 + 20` restoration corridor |
| `46 BC → AD 25` | `70` | `40 + 30` through the `6 BC` hinge |

These rows are of unequal historical status and remain appendix-only.

The prior `62/62` arithmetic and `16/16` claim-control audit remains source history for the Enochic `49` and Christ-envelope portions; its Luke-`77` subset is superseded by the present bounded Luke-`76` correction.

### K.1 Double-generation blocks

| Carrier | Joshua block | Enochic block | Jesus block | Block length |
|---|---|---|---|---:|
| Primary `70` | `1476→1406→1336 BC` | `3436→3366→3296 BC` | `6 BC→AD 65→AD 135` | `140=2×70` |
| Companion `72` | `1520→1448→1376 BC` | `3536→3464→3392 BC` | `8 BC→AD 65→AD 137` | `144=2×72` |

The Joshua and Enochic blocks occupy different translation depths even though both terminate on the same Jesus double-generation block.

### K.2 Joshua `21`-generation corresponding-boundary translations

| Carrier | Left boundary | Hinge | Right boundary | Common span |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `70` | `1476 BC→6 BC` | `1406 BC→AD 65` | `1336 BC→AD 135` | `1470=3×490=21×70` |
| `72` | `1520 BC→8 BC` | `1448 BC→AD 65` | `1376 BC→AD 137` | `1512=3×504=21×72` |

The carrier difference is:

```text
1512 − 1470
= 42
= 3 × 14
= 21 × 2.
```

The count is:

```text
3 weeks × 7 generations
= 21 generations.
```

### K.3 Enochic `49`-generation corresponding-boundary translations

#### Primary `70`

```text
3436 BC → 3366 BC → 3296 BC
    ↓3430      ↓3430      ↓3430
  6 BC →    AD 65 →    AD 135
```

```text
3430
= 49 × 70
= 7 × 490.
```

#### Companion `72`

```text
3536 BC → 3464 BC → 3392 BC
    ↓3528      ↓3528      ↓3528
  8 BC →    AD 65 →    AD 137
```

```text
3528
= 49 × 72
= 7 × 504.
```

The Enochic count is:

```text
7 weeks × 7 generations
= 49 generations.
```

### K.4 Luke-derived `76`-generation corresponding-boundary translation

| Lukan opening boundary | Jesus corresponding boundary | Span |
|---|---|---:|
| figurative God pre-head `5326 BC` | Christ birth hinge `6 BC` | `5320=76×70` |
| Adam head `5256 BC` | Jesus first-generation close `AD 65` | `5320=76×70` |
| Adam-generation close `5186 BC` | Jesus second-generation close `AD 135` | `5320=76×70` |

The ordered blocks are:

```text
5326 BC → 5256 BC → 5186 BC
    ↓5320      ↓5320      ↓5320
  6 BC →    AD 65 →    AD 135
```

```text
5320
= 76 × 70.
```

The three boundaries on each side advance by one `70`-year generation. File_68 records only this exact translation. The explanation of why Luke's backward schematic motion yields `76` rather than `77` is developed in Final File_69.

### K.5 The inverse terminal `−110` Christ envelope

The generated second-generation closes project backward by the inverse rail:

```text
AD 135 − 110 = AD 25
AD 137 − 110 = AD 27.
```

The complete head envelope is:

```text
8 BC → 6 BC → AD 25 → AD 27
   2        30         2

2 + 30 + 2 = 34.
```

The middle `30` is the primary civil span:

```text
6 BC → AD 25
= 30.
```

The companion head and close supply the two-year outer flanks. This is a cross-rail envelope, not one ordinary generation.

### K.6 Forwarded seven-year endpoints

The Creation-week envelope is advanced seven elapsed years from every Christ endpoint:

| Carrier | No-rail head | Forwarded close | Inverse-rail head | Forwarded close |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Primary `70` | `6 BC` | `AD 2` | `AD 25` | `AD 32` |
| Companion `72` | `8 BC` | `1 BC` | `AD 27` | `AD 34` |

Thus the furthest endpoints are:

```text
no rail:       1 BC / AD 2
inverse rail:  AD 32 / AD 34.
```

They preserve the same envelope:

```text
1 BC → AD 2 → AD 32 → AD 34
  2         30         2

2 + 30 + 2 = 34.
```

No year zero is used. The `+7` closes are week-of-years endpoints, not carrier termini or Passion dates.

### K.7 The `21/49/76` non-collapse hierarchy

| Layer | Count | Primary span | Companion span | Local burden |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Joshua | `21=3×7` | `1470=21×70` | `1512=21×72` | three-week overlapping field |
| Enoch | `49=7×7` | `3430=49×70` | `3528=49×72` | seven-week-core field |
| Luke | `76` | `5320=76×70` | deferred | backward God/Adam double-generation translation |

The `21`, `49`, and `77` counts are nested comparative depths. They are not interchangeable and do not constitute three independent textual genealogies.

### K.8 Claim ceiling and File_69 boundary

- `AD 65` remains the sole translated seven-week terminus.
- `AD 135/137` are post-terminal second-generation closes.
- `AD 25/27` are inverse-rail comparison nodes, not carrier termini.
- `1 BC/AD 2` and `AD 32/34` are forwarded seven-year closes, not Passion dates.
- The Joshua `21`, Enoch `49`, and Luke `76` counts remain distinct.
- The `72` Joshua and Enochic boundaries are schematic companion coordinates, not historical biography anchors.
- The inverse `−110` endpoint projection is not a fifth rail or a global chronology correction.
- Final File_69 executes the full Luke/Matthew transfer. File_68 imports no generated File_69 coordinate as proof and should require only bounded reciprocal maintenance unless a substantive contradiction emerges.
